Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on electrical structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 398. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 700 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 398 + O(1/n^700). Summing over all modes and applying the electrical convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
